# Skills · P · part 10

832 entries, panspermia-mutation to paper-covert. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **panspermia-mutation**: Use for a fan-out where the obvious framing of the problem is suspect — mutate each worker's framing, run them blind, and select the fittest. Variation-and-selection on top of the genome layer. Not for single dispatches (no selection step) or routine fan-outs (plain dispatch suffices). ([Sarb0Z/colloid-swarm](https://github.com/Sarb0Z/colloid-swarm/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/panspermia-mutation))

- **pantheon**: A skill that runs a hard, testable coding task through a multi-agent harness instead of a single model pass (base variant, no external model needed). - **pantheon-fix**: A skill that FIXES an existing bug or gap in a real repo through a multi-agent harness instead of a single patch pass. It records a test baseline, then builds N candidate fixes in parallel — each in its own isolated git worktree with a failing repro test — gated on "nothing that passed before now fails" + "the repro test now passes". Adversarial reviewers (Claude) then try to break each fix and a judge picks the smallest safe patch; if they break all of them, NOTHING wins. Outputs a diff and never touches your working tree unless apply:true. Pairs with pantheon-gap. - **paper-antonucci-2020-solar-orbiter-metis-coronagraph**: Use when retrieving Solar Orbiter / METIS visible-light polarized brightness (VL 580–640 nm) and Lyman-α HI 121.6 nm coronal imagery — central claim is that METIS is the first dual-channel (VL + HI Ly-α) coronagraph providing co- temporal H I outflow speed and polarized-brightness density maps from 1.7 R☉ to ~9 R☉ (Antonucci et al. 2020, A&A). ([huangzesen/heliosi-501-corpus-skill](https://github.com/huangzesen/heliosi-501-corpus-skill/tree/HEAD/references/corpus/wave500_instruments_data_software_045/paper-antonucci-2020-solar-orbiter-metis-coronagraph))
- **paper-appraisal**: Use whenever the user hands over a paper or study (arXiv, DOI, PubMed, journal or PDF link) to review, appraise, summarise, explain or present, in any field: medicine and health, psychology and cognitive science, mechanistic biology, ML. Use it even when the main deliverable is slides, a video or an explainer page. Also to review a paper like a scientist or peer reviewer (referee report), to read a paper for someone who is not a scientist, or to teach how to read one. Also for judging evidence: 'is this study good', 'what does the research say', risk of bias, GRADE, systematic and literature… ([samhvw8/dotfiles](https://github.com/samhvw8/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/home/.ccp/hub/skills/paper-appraisal) · ★15)
- **paper-appraisal**: Use when a user asks for a journal-club review, critical appraisal or methodological assessment of a specific physical therapy or rehabilitation research paper identified by URL, title, PMID, DOI, attachment or a returned article record. ([jallad91/physiokeys-claude-plugin](https://github.com/jallad91/physiokeys-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-appraisal))
- **paper-arch-figure**: 画论文 / 讲解用的神经网络架构图（DiT、MMDiT、LLM 解码层、MoE、编码器-解码器、U-Net、多阶段扩散流水线、图生图 / 编辑的条件接入）。默认版式固定为论文式三段放大：左段整体数据流、中段一个 block 内部、右段独有机制，主干一律自下而上，块内只放短标签；风格是五色语义配色、圆角无描边块、虚线放大、蓝框标独有做法。先从源码取证并记行号，再从金样板 spec 改出 Python spec，生成可编辑的 draw.io，导出 SVG / PNG，与金样板并排自查。用户说「画模型架构图」「画论文结构图」「把 block 内部画出来」「重画这张架构图」「用 draw.io 画模型结构」时用。不用于系统 / 基础设施 / 数据流水线架构图（那是 archify 或 drawio-skill），也不用于数据图表。 ([ManagerZhang10/algorithm-engineer-skills](https://github.com/ManagerZhang10/algorithm-engineer-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/paper-arch-figure) · ★1)
- **paper-architect**: Multi-stage paper output flow — venue setup, direction discussion, paper scouting, problem focusing, motivation experiment design, section drafting. Organizes everything under outputs/papers/&lt;venue&gt;/&lt;direction&gt;/. Use when the user is starting or working on a paper for a specific venue. Triggered by /paper. ([xxx1766/lucky-research](https://github.com/xxx1766/lucky-research/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/paper-architect) · ★2)
- **paper-architect**: Structure an academic paper before drafting — find the motivation spine (the gap it fills), build the argument tree with every claim mapped to evidence, choose the section structure (IMRaD or thematic), plan the citations, and set boundary conditions. Use when the user says "structure my paper", "outline this paper", "what's my argument", "help me frame this study", or "turn my results into a paper". ([MalloyTheDev/awesomeplugin](https://github.com/MalloyTheDev/awesomeplugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/researchkit/skills/paper-architect))
- **paper-architect**: Builds the paper skeleton: headings, subheadings, and bullet-point intentions for each section. Use this skill when the user says 'outline my paper,' 'build the skeleton,' 'create paper structure,' 'what sections do I need,' 'harden the skeleton,' or anything about defining the structural outline of a manuscript. Trigger after the scope-strategist has defined the paper's strategy. Blends the user's strategy with role model paper designs and study-specific details to create a unique but well-patterned structure.
